# META
~~~ini
description=A feature chain after selection with a callback resolves through the selection result; callback parameters retain their own scope (KerML 8.2.5.8.3 and 8.3.4.8.18).
type=file
libraries=standard
require_no_diagnostics=true
require_complete_publication=true
~~~
# SOURCE
~~~sysml
package Selection {
    private import ScalarValues::*;
    private import ControlFunctions::*;
    attribute def E { attribute v : Real; }
    calc def T {
        in xs : E [0..*];
        return : Real [0..*] = xs->select { in x : E; x.v > 0.0 }.v;
    }
    calc def Nested {
        in xs : E [0..*];
        return : Real = xs->select { in x : E; x.v > 0.0 }#(1).v;
    }
}
~~~
